CABINET SECRETARIAT 
NOTIFICATION 

New Delhi, the 25th September, 1985 

S.O. 696(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred 
by clause (3) of article 77 of the Constitution, the 
President hereby makes the following rules further to 
amend the Government of India (Allocation of 
Business) Rules, 1961, namely :— 

"1. (1) These rules may be called the Government 
of India (Allocation of Business) (One hundred and 
Seventy-fourth Amendment) Rules, 1985. 

(2) They shall come into force at once. 

" 2. In the Government of India (Allocation of 
Business) Rules, 1961, for the Schedules the following 
Schedules shall be substituted, namely :— 

“THE FIRST SCHEDULE 
(Rule 2)
